115年 9月全國賦稅收入初步統計 
一、全國賦稅收入 
（一）本月數 
9 月實徵淨額 4,965 億元，較上年同月增加 813 億元(+19.6%)。 
（二）累計數 
累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 3 兆 5,714 億元，較上年同期增加 7,004 億
元(+24.4%)。 
（三）達成率 
1.中央政府總預算業於 9 月 18 日公布，稅課收入較原預算案提高
6,000 億元，併計地方政府及特種基金後，全國稅課收入較預算案
提高 5,852 億元，本次發布均改依法定數計算預算達成率(實徵淨
額占全年預算數)。 
2.累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額占累計分配預算數 115.0%，占全年預算數
78.8%。 
圖 1 全國賦稅收入及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至 6 月底所致。

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）9 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營業稅增加 297 億元、營利事業
所得稅增加 254 億元及證券交易稅增加 222 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,077
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 1,853 億元及營業稅增加 1,203 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-9 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)9 月實徵淨額 2,007 億元，較上年同月增加 254 億元(+14.5%)，主
因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 1 兆 28 億元，較上年同期增加 1,853 億元
(+22.7%)，主因結算申報自繳 、暫繳稅款 俱增及延分期稅款陸續
入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
9 月實徵淨額 490 億元，較上年同月增加 34 億元(+7.4%)，主因扣繳
稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 8,279 億元，較上年同期增加
790 億元(+10.6%)，主因結算申報自繳增加，以及盈餘分配、薪資所

得等扣繳稅款均呈成長。
3.營業稅
9月實徵淨額1,414億元，較上年同月增加297億元(+26.6%)，累計1至
9月實徵淨額6,230億元，較上年同期增加1,203億元(+23.9%)，均因內
需穩定，加以電子零組件 、資通與視聽產品 等進口代徵稅額增加 ，
惟出口暢旺，適用外銷零稅率營業人申請退稅亦增，抵銷部分增幅。

### Page 3

4.貨物稅
(1)9月實徵淨額100億元，較上年同月減少11億元(-10.0%)，主因汽柴
油減徵幅度擴大，惟電動車大量到港推升進口 車稅額，抵銷部分
減幅。
(2)累計1至9月實徵淨額874億元，較上年同期減少207億元(-19.2%)，
除汽柴油降稅因素外 ，亦與新購汽機車及電動車之減免徵退稅增
加有關。
5.證券交易稅
(1)9 月及累計 1 至 9 月上市上櫃股票平均每日成交值為 1 兆 317 億
元、1 兆 1,461 億元，分別較上年同期(6,006 億元、4,451 億元)增
71.8%、1.6 倍。
(2)9 月 證 交 稅 實 徵 淨 額534 億 元， 較 上 年 同 月 增 加 222 億 元
(+71.2%)；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 5,060 億元，較上年同期增加
3,077 億元(+1.6 倍)。
6.關稅
9 月實徵淨額 157 億元，較上年同月增加 3 億元(+1.9%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 1,224 億元，較上年同期增加 80 億元(+7.0%)，均因機械

及電機設備、基本金屬及其製品等進口稅額增加。
7.贈與稅
9 月實徵淨額 23 億元，較上年同月增加 6 億元(+35.7%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 211 億元，較上年同期增加 43 億元(+25.3%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加。
8.土地增值稅
9 月實徵淨額 56 億元，較上年同月增加 12 億元(+26.6%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 504 億元，較上年同期減少
14 億元(-2.8%)，主因房地交易量及大額稅款案件減少。
9.印花稅
9 月實徵淨額 31 億元，較上年同月增加 4 億元(+16.2%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 175 億元，較上年同期增加 15 億元(+9.0%)，主因銀行放
款稅額成長。
（三）前 9月主要稅目實徵淨額占分配預算數之比率：
1.大於 100%者有證交稅(272%)、遺產稅(155%)、贈與稅(132%)、印花
稅(130%)、營業稅(116%)、關稅(114%)、契稅(111%)、房屋稅(106%)、

綜所稅(101%)、牌照稅(101%)及營所稅(101%)等。
2.低於 100%者有貨物稅(98%)、菸酒稅(97%)、土增稅(85%)、地價稅
(84%)及特銷稅(57%)等。

三、各級政府賦稅收入概況
(一)中央政府
累計 1 至 9 月中央政府賦稅收入 (不含中央統籌分配稅款 )實徵淨 額
2 兆 3,603 億元(占分配預算數 119.3%)，較上年同期增加 2,553 億元
(+12.1%)，雖新版財政收支劃分法 開始施行，營業稅劃歸中央政府 比
率大幅調降，減少 2,748 億元(-90.7%)，惟證交稅、所得稅分別增加 3,077
億元、2,230 億元，互抵後呈現上升。
(二)地方政府
累計 1 至 9 月地方政府賦稅收
入(含中央統籌分配稅款 )實徵
淨額 1 兆 898 億元(占分配預
算數 109.4%)，較上年同期增
加 4,527 億元(+71.0%)，主因
納入中央統籌分配 之營業稅
增加 4,030 億元(+2.1 倍)所致。
(三)中央特種基金
累計 1 至 9 月撥入中央特種基金之健康福利捐實徵淨額 195 億元，較
上年同期增加 2 億元(+0.8%)；房地合一課徵所得稅撥入住宅基金 135
億元，較上年同期減少 7 億元(-4.7%)；撥入長照基金 883 億元(其中 541

億元來自房地合一課徵所得稅、195 億元來自菸稅，146 億元來自遺贈
稅)，較上年同期增加 8 億元(+0.9%)。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 聯準會上周三決議升息一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，FOMC 政策聲明顯示，18 名
FOMC 成員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次 ， 另有4 人認為存在升息兩次的可
能性 ，加上聯準會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)會後表達控制通膨的偏鷹立場 ， 帶動美
元指數走升至 100 高位。
⚫ 歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數
據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解 、 能源價格持續高位情況下 ， 市場對於歐元區經
濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度。
⚫ 美國公布初請失業金人數為 19.6 萬人，優於市場預期的 20.8 萬，再度呼應聯準
會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)認為目前勞動力市場仍具韌性的說法， 在美國經濟穩
健及通膨頑固的情況下，預計聯準會將持續升息，有利美元持穩。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委暨葡萄牙央行總裁佩雷拉(Alvaro Santos Pereira)表示，歐元區目前

通膨雖未廣泛擴散 ， 惟考量近期天然氣價格飆升 ， 央行將密切關注未來通膨壓力
擴散情形。最新利率期貨市場顯示，歐洲央行今年底仍有再升息一碼空間。
⚫ 日本政府通過消費稅減稅及家庭現金補貼的政策大綱，預計自明年 4 月起的兩
年內，將食品消費稅率自 8%降至 1%，惟大綱中未明確說明資金來源，再度引
發市場對於日本政府的財政擔憂，短線不利日圓表現。
⚫ 台灣央行 9/17 宣布重貼現率連 10 凍維持在 2%不變，央行總裁楊金龍表示，台
灣的通膨相較其他主要經濟體溫和，採取漸進緊縮貨幣政策有助物價穩定。 此
外，目前通膨率為 2%，明年有望降至 2%以下，後續將觀察通膨發展、主要經
濟體貨幣政策緊縮程度及台灣金融情勢，以決定未來貨幣政策走向。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.650，低點在 31.958，以 31.808 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶 ， 美國CPI 數據公布後加強市場對於聯準會 9 月升息的預期 ，
早盤台幣跳空開低，外資連續三日賣超台股並匯出，加以出口商態度轉為惜售，令
台幣接連貶破 31.700、31.800、31.900 關卡， 週四由於聯準會偏鷹派升息，美元指
數走升至 100.300，早盤台幣跳空開低，台幣最低貶至31.958，不過隨後出口商賣壓
出籠，外資雙向進出，台幣收復部分跌幅，維持在31.900 附近震盪；臨近周末，外
資買超台股並於外匯市場匯入 ， 台幣走升至31.722， 最終在央行調節之下收在31.808。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場而言 ， 美國最新初領失業救濟金人數較預
期減少一萬多人、續領失業救濟金人數亦下降，顯示美國勞動力市場尚屬穩健，加
以聯準會升息抗通膨，鷹派立場給予美元指數支撐、不利台幣表現；就台灣市場來
看，九月台灣央行並未升息，台美利差擴大預計將帶給台幣壓力，此外，儘管聯準
會升息利空出盡，外資買超台股帶動台幣走升，惟台灣即將迎來中秋連假，預計金
融市場交投謹慎，將令台幣陷入橫盤整理格局，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢區間
震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/21/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走升。人民幣中間價連續走升，接連升破 6.7700、6.7600 整數
關口，升至三年七個月來高點，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美元指數震盪走強，加
以市場靜待聯準會利率決議公布 ， 交投謹慎 ， 人民幣升幅有限 ， 大致維持高檔震盪 ，
週四聯準會鷹派升息一碼，惟人民幣中間價連續五日走升至 6.7521，並創下四年高
點，帶動人民幣緩步走升，USD/CNH 升破 6.7000 整數大關，終場境外人民幣收在
6.6944，週線升值 94 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6984，週線升值 21 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣震盪走升 。 就國際市場來看 ，FED 最新政策聲明和經濟預測顯
示，聯準會為明年繼續收緊貨幣政策敞開了大門，美元指數走升，抑制人民幣升值
的速度；就中國國內市場來看，中間價持續偏升將帶動人民幣走升，加以出口商實
質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現； 最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中 ， 匯價維持
在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，加以USD/CNH 有效跌破 6.7000 整數關
卡，顯示 USD/CNH 空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪走升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6700-6.7200

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走貶。 週初，美國通膨數據維持高位 ，令聯準會升息預期升溫 ，USD/JPY
自 153.29 低點反彈至 155 附近；週間 ，美國聯準會宣布升息一碼 ， 帶動USD/JPY 上
漲至 156.41；臨近週末，日本央行如市場預期升息一碼，惟央行偏鴿派立場令日圓
續貶，帶動 USD/JPY 一度最高來到 158.05，而隨後在日本政府於外匯市場進行匯率
詢價下，USD/JPY 回吐部分漲幅，終場收在 156.86，週線上漲 349 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本政府上周通過消費稅減
稅及家庭現金補貼政策大綱，惟大綱中未明確說明財源，再度引發市場對於日本政
府財政惡化的擔憂，預計將不利日圓走勢。二、日本央行上周五決議將政策利率調
升一碼至 1.25%， 9 名決策委員中有 2 人對升息持反對意見，降低市場對日本央行
進一步升息的預期。另一方面 ， 聯準會上周三亦升息一碼至3.75%-4.00%區間 ，FOMC
政策聲明顯示，18 位官員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次，另有 4 人認為存在升
息兩次的可能性，聯準會升息立場明顯較日本央行更為鷹派，預計將支撐USD/JPY
走勢。三、日本財務大臣片山皋月近期再度重申，將於必要時採取外匯干預行動，
以維護外匯市場有序運行。四、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 短中天期均
線向下發散走勢偏空，惟近期匯價在多次向下測試 154 均拉回，明顯下方有買盤支
撐。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：154.80-159.50

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，聯準會升息預期升溫加上歐元區 9 月經濟景氣指
數下滑，令歐元走跌至 1.1525；週間，美國聯準會鷹派升息立場帶動美元走升，歐
元最低來到 1.1460；臨近週末，歐元持續偏弱整理，終場收在 1.1483，週線下跌 107
點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、 歐洲央行管委卡薩克斯
(Martins Kazaks) 表示 ， 為避免美伊戰爭引發的燃料成本上漲開始傳導至工資和其他
商品價格，歐洲央行可能需要漸進式調升利率以抑制通膨。最新利率期貨市場顯示
歐洲央行今年仍有升息一碼的空間，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國聯準會決議將
利率調升一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，聯準會主席華許會後偏鷹言論帶動美元指數走
升至 100 上方，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自
前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解、能源
價格持續高位情況下，市場對於歐元區經濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度，將不利歐元
表現。四、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 近期回測年線未能有效突破後明顯
拉回轉為下行走勢，目前匯價承壓於各天期均線下方，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週央行理監事會議決議結果 ， 維持政策利率不變 ， 符合
市場預期 ， 且近兩週央行持續減發定存單 ， 令市場短錢充裕 ， 惟週五銀行間分
配略為不均，部分銀行有資金需求，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.70%，利率高位較上週上升 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投清淡，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三等殖系統成交在 1.86%，10 年
期指標券 A15106 則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.92~1.921%。 
台灣央行理監事會議如市場預期維持政策利率不變 ， 總裁楊金龍表示 ， 今年國
內通膨仍屬可控，明年有望回落至 2%以下，市場對今年升息疑慮有所降溫，
對台債形成支撐 ， 惟國際債市走勢仍偏空 ， 預期台債殖利率短線仍呈高檔區間
盤整格局，後續關注下週三財政部公布的第四季公債發行計劃，預估 5 年券
區間為 1.79%~1.83%，10 年券區間為 1.90%~1.95%。

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 看好記憶體需求緊俏有利報價走升，維持「買進」 投資評等，目標價 800 元(6 x 27(F)PER)，
潛在上漲空間 51.8%。

2026/10/12 收盤價： 527.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 1336.5 億元新台幣， QoQ+61.9%，YoY+ 
611.7%，毛利率 85.7% ，營業費用 75.88 億元，營業利
益為 1068.9億元 ， 業外收入24.38億 ， 稅後淨利為 876.3
億元﹐EPS 為 25.4 元，每股淨值為 118.99元。營收成
長及毛利雙雙成長受惠 平均銷售單價季增超過 六十位數
百分比，而季減中個位數百分比，匯率有利低個位數百分
比。 
➢ 展望 26Q4，受惠 AI 需求續強預期 DRAM 將持續缺貨，
且南亞科 AI相關產品已佔營收逾 20%，雖受限產能滿載
出貨量將無法再提升，然而預期合約價將持續於 26Q4上
漲 15~20%，營收及毛利率將持續創高。因此兆豐預估營
收 1556.04 億元，QoQ+16.4%，YoY+417.1%，毛利率 
88.1%，EPS為 30.35 元。 
➢ 展望 2026~2027 年，受惠 AI 需求持續強勁三大原廠於
DRAM產能持續滿載，預期 DDR5、DDR4、DDR3等產
品將持續供不應求。1C 產品預期於 27Q1 開始驗證，有
利打入 AI伺服器供應鏈，預期 AI 占比將逐季提高。因此
兆豐預估營收 4208.9/6993.1 億元，YoY+532.1/66.2%，

營 運 分 析

南亞科技為全球前四大 DRAM廠商 
南亞科技主要致力 DRAM (動態隨機存取記憶體)的研發、設計、製造與銷售，
開發自主技術，發展智慧財產權，培育關鍵技術研發團隊，落實綠色科技及
智慧製造全面提升製程效能與產能，是全球第4大的 DRAM公司。公司已成
功開發 10 奈米級 DRAM 製程技術，將可微縮至少 3 個世代，並用以發展
DDR5及 LPDDR5 等下世代產品，強化公司自主技術開發與製程轉換佈局。
第二世代 10 奈米級製程技術(1B)產品 16Gb DDR5 已完成設計與試產，於
2024 年量產。南亞科技具備完整 DRAM 產品線，可因應智慧世代的發展，
滿足多元化的產品需求。預期 1C產品於 27Q1 開始驗證。

85.7% ，營業費用 75.88 億元，營業利益為 1068.9 億元，業外收入 24.38
億，稅後淨利為 876.3 億元﹐EPS 為 25.4 元，每股淨值為 118.99元。營

中個位數百分比，匯率有利低個位數百分比。

26Q4合約價持續上漲

品已佔營收逾 20%，雖受限產能滿載出貨量將無法再提升，然而預期合約價
將持續於 26Q4 上漲 15~20%，營收及毛利率將持續創高。因此兆豐預估營
收 1556.04 億元，QoQ+16.4%，YoY+417.1%，毛利率 88.1%，EPS 為 30.35
元。

個股報告

圖三、DRAM合約價報價走勢圖

資料來源：兆豐投顧整理

南亞科完成 787.2 億元私募案，攜手四大客戶搶佔 AI 供應鏈先機 
南亞科日前完成總額新台幣  787.2 億元的私募認購，吸引四大核心客戶參
與。增資後，相關投資方持股比例達 10.19%。雙方同時簽署供應協議，確
保未來能穩定供應多元 DRAM 產品組合。主要目的在於強化南亞科與客戶
的戰略合作，透過 e-SSD 與網通產品等領域，建立 AI 及雲端服務供應商
（CSP）供應鏈中的雙贏局面。私募資金將注資新廠以提升產能，進一步確
立南亞科在 AI 價值鏈中的市場地位。本次私募後，南亞科總發行股數將達 
34.5 億股。

記憶體供不應求，產能持續擴充 
目前產能為 6.5 萬片/月，產能利用率維持滿載，2026Q1~Q3 資本支出 276
億，2026 以不超過 697 億元為上限，設備佔比五成．5A新廠第一階段 35,900
片/月 於 2026~2029 資本支出約 3,466 億．新廠預計於 2027 年初開始裝機，
主要生產 1B/1C/1D製程，預期 27年底開出 20k/月，2028 年達每月投片量
達 30k/月，主要因應網通、eSSD等產品之強勁需求。

AI 帶動記憶體強勁需求，有利報價持續走升 
展望 2026~2027 年，受惠 AI 需求持續強勁三大原廠於 DRAM 產能持續滿
載，預期DDR5、DDR4、DDR3等產品將持續供不應求。1C產品預期於 27Q1
開始驗證，有利打入 AI 伺服器供應鏈，預期 AI 占比將逐季提高。因此兆豐
預估營收 4208.9/6993.1 億元，YoY+532.1/66.2%，毛利率83.3/86.3%，EPS
為 77.85/133.2 元。

記憶體持續供不應
求，報價持續走升。 
2027 新產能主要 用
於生產 1B/1C/1D 製
程。 
大型私募案確保未來
穩定供應多元 DRAM

南亞科(2408) 
買進, TP 710 (潛在報酬 45.49%) 
買進 
Price(10/13):488 
Target Price：710

展望未來，南亞科(2408.TT) 因受限於極低庫存，2027 年上半年
位元出貨量預期將保持平穩，待 4Q27 5A 新廠開出產能後，出貨
量將恢復逐步成長；產能方面，5A 新廠規劃於 2027 年第一季開
始進場裝機，第一階段預計 2028 年底前投片量達 3 萬片，全產
能 5 萬片目標於 2030 年底達成，且擴建嚴格以客戶 LTA 的 AI
專案為導向；此外，公司規劃於屏東科學園區建置3D 晶圓封裝與
測試廠，預計 2028 下半年導入設備，並與客戶合作開發客製化  
AI/WoW(晶圓堆疊)與 ASIC 專案，已開始產生部分營收貢獻，長
期目標於 2029 至 2030 年將 AI 基礎設施營收占比拉高至 40%
至 50%；技術面上，次世代 1C 製程預計於 2027 年第一季進行
客戶驗證 。 綜合以上 ， 研究部預估南亞科2027 年 EPS 為 130.72
元。考量目前公司記憶體供給吃緊、需求擴大，有長期合約支持未
來成長 ，且公司具備獨特製程升級與客製化產品線成長潛力 ， 研究
部給予 5.5 倍本益比評價，目標價 710 元，投資建議為買進。

⚫ 公司基本資料 南亞科(2408.TT)公司簡介 
南亞科技(2408.TT)成立於 1995 年，隸屬於台塑集團，為全球領先的動
態隨機存取記憶體(DRAM)製造大廠 。 公司生產基地主要位於台灣 ， 擁有
多樣化的產品組合，涵蓋 DDR5、LPDDR5/5X、DDR4、LPDDR4/4X、
DDR3 及 LPDDR3 等產品線 ， 產品種類達數十顆 ， 服務全球數百家客戶 。
面對傳統記憶體產業之劇烈循環波動，公司近年積極推動技術升級與結
構性轉型，除中長期積極投入 1B(16 奈米)與 1C(12 奈米)先進製程轉換
及新五廠建置，並深入布局企業級 SSD(eSSD)、SmartNIC、BMC 等
AI 伺服器基礎設施，同時與客戶合作開發客製化 AI/WoW(晶圓堆疊)利
基產品，正逐步由傳統大宗商品化DRAM 廠，轉型為具備高附加價值與
高敏捷度的全方位利基型記憶體解決方案供應商 。

2Q26 營運表現全面成長，售價調漲驅動獲利大幅提升 
南 亞 科 (2408.TT) 2Q26 營 業 收 入 為 825.49 億元 (Q+68.17% / 
Y+684.23%) ， 營收展現強勁成長 ， 受惠於平均銷售單價(ASP)季增超過
60%的漲勢，有效抵消了位元出貨量與上季持平的量能限制；而在 ASP 
大幅上漲且成本結構維持穩定的推升下，單季毛利率提升至
79.49%(Q+11.60ppt / Y+100.05ppt) 。雖然員工分紅與銷售研發使營業
股本(億) 345.0

殖利率(%) 0.32 費用增加至 47.93 億元 ， 但配合業外淨收益15.03 億元的挹注(貢獻 EPS
約 0.44 元)，使整體利潤大幅提升，獲利表現優於市場預期。綜合以上，
南亞科 (2408.TT)2Q26 營業收入為  825.49 億 元 ，
Q+68.17%/Y+684.23%、毛利率 79.49%，Q+11.60ppt/Y+100.05ppt、
營業利益率 73.68%，Q+12.34ppt/Y+116.44ppt、稅後純益 501.92 億
元，Q+92.61%/Y+虧轉盈、EPS 為 14.66 元 。

3Q26 獲利大幅跳升，低價長約重簽驅動 ASP 季增逾六成 
南亞科(2408.TT) 3Q26 營收 1,336.49 億元(Q+61.9% / Y+611.7%)，雖
然出貨量因公司整體庫存已降至極低水位，呈中個位數季減，但整體營
收受惠於平均銷售單價(ASP)季增超過 60%(年增超過 500%)的強勁漲
勢；ASP 強勢上揚的核心主因，在於早期簽訂的低價長期合約 (LTA)於
第三季集中到期並重新議價(對 ASP 季增貢獻度超過半數)。在 ASP 大
幅 上 漲 且 生 產 成 本 結 構 穩 定 的 推 升 下 ， 單 季 毛 利 率 跳 升 至  
85.7%(Q+6.2ppt / Y+67.2ppt) 。費用方面，因增發員工薪酬獎勵，研發
費用增加至 51.45 億元、銷管費用增加至 24.43 億元，合計營業費用
為 75.88 億元 。 綜合以上 ， 南亞科(2408.TT) 3Q26 營業收入為 1,336.49
億元， Q+61.90%/Y+611.70% 、 毛 利 率  85.70% ，
Q+6.21ppt/Y+67.25ppt、 營業利益率 79.98%，Q+6.30ppt/Y+74.02ppt、
稅後純益 876.27 億元，Q+74.58%/Y+虧轉盈、EPS 為 25.40 元。

2026 年受惠 DRAM 報價持續上漲與 AI 伺服器需求，獲利創歷史新高 
展望 2026 年全年，南亞科(2408.TT) 前三季累計淨利已高達 1,639 億
元，累計EPS 達 49.28 元，公司維持 2026 全年位元銷售量成長高十
位數百分比的目標；合約與定價機制上，公司目前長約(LTA)覆蓋率落在 
50% 至 70% 之間，其中 3 年期以上長約占總出貨量 20% 以上，履
約率高達 100%，預期第四季 DRAM 供需緊缺狀態延續，合約平均售
價保持溫和成長；需求端方面，GPU、TPU、CPU 及 ASIC 等 AI 基
礎建設建置強勁，推升 AI 伺服器與基礎設施相關產品之營收占比突破 
20%； 產品組合中 DDR4 與 LPDDR4 合計營收占比超過 60%，DDR5 
占比約 10%。管理層指引目前產業短期內難以緩解供給壓力，隨著第四
季 DRAM ASP 持續看漲 ， 公司毛利率表現有機會再進一步拉升 。 綜合以
上，研究部預估南亞科(2408.TT) 2026 年營業收入為 4,190.95 億元，
Y+529.40%、毛利率  82.61%，Y+60.10ppt、營業利益率  76.83%，
Y+68.96ppt、稅後純益 2,642.16 億元，Y+3,894.93%、EPS 為 76.58
元。

長鑫存儲(CXMT)技術進展與產業影響 
長鑫存儲(CXMT)於 2026 年 9 月宣佈第五代 DRAM 制程(G5)進入量產階
段。儘管目前 G5 產品仍處於客戶驗證與良率提升階段，距離大規模商
業化出貨尚需時間 ， 但已顯示中國DRAM 技術發展速度明顯超出市場原
先預期。 長鑫 HBM3E 良率已達 40%左右。市場普遍認為，長鑫在無法
取得 EUV 設備的情況下，主要透過 ArF DUV 浸潤式曝光搭配多重曝光
(Multiple Patterning) 技術推進制程微縮，因此雖然能夠達到接近國際大
廠先進 DRAM 節點的設計能力 ， 但製造成本 、 良率與生產效率仍與三星 、
SK 海力士及美光存在差距。未來能否持續提升至國際大廠 70%以上的
量產水準，將是觀察長鑫HBM 競爭力的重要指標。短期而言，長鑫技術
進步對台廠商影響有限，長鑫目前仍面臨良率提升與客戶驗證所需時
間，短期內難以快速增加有效供給，因此全球 DRAM 供應緊張的局面仍
將延續至 2027 至 2028 年。相較之下，三星、SK 海力士與美光持續將
產能轉向 HBM 產品，反而造成 DDR4 供給逐漸退出市場，進一步支撐
DDR4 價格維持強勢，有利於以 DDR4 產品為主的南亞科營運表現；然
而，中長期則需注意，長鑫 DRAM 投片量將於 2027 年底達到約 30K 
WPM，並持續擴產，若未來 G5 良率持續改善、HBM 技術逐步成熟，
加上中國市場本土化政策支持，長鑫未來在中低階 DDR5、PC DRAM
及中國本土市場的競爭力可能快速提升 ， 進而對全球DRAM 供需格局產
生實質影響。

長鑫存儲(CXMT)4F²技術 
長鑫存儲在 10 月 12 日有表示年底推出 4F²架構 DDR5 伺服器記憶體，
4F²是將每個記憶體儲存單元配置在面積相當於 4 倍 F 平方的區域內 ， 其
中 F 代表半導體制程的最小線寬，這種設計能讓同一顆晶片容納更多儲
存單元，進而提高記憶體容量。目前，主流商用 DRAM 仍採用 6F 平方
儲存單元結構。長鑫存儲表示，其4F 平方設計的核心，在於自主研發的
垂直環繞閘極(Vertical Surrounding Gate，VSG)電晶體，這項技術透過
垂直閘極架構改善漏電問題，同時大幅縮小單元面積，提高儲存密度。
SK 海力士(SK Hynix) 已於 2025 年公佈 4F 平方技術發展藍圖，另外，
根據南韓媒體今年 4 月的報導，三星電子(Samsung Electronics)計畫於
2028 年前，量產採用這項新設計、制程低於 10 奈米的 DRAM。

結論與建議 
展望未來 ， 南亞科(2408.TT) 因受限於極低庫存 ，2027 年上半年位元出
貨量預期將保持平穩 ， 待4Q27 5A 新廠開出產能後 ， 出貨量將恢復逐步
成長；產能方面，5A 新廠規劃於 2027 年第一季開始進場裝機，第一

Largan Precision  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 Results Miss on Lower
GPM, but FAU/CPO
Development Well on Track
Largan reported 3Q26 net income of NT$5.182bn, down 27% YoY.
The company reported a 11% YoY revenue decline, to NT$15.651bn; gross margin
declined YoY and QoQ to 42.2% in 3Q26, lower than expected due to lower new-
project yields and structural green electricity cost hikes.
Management expects shipments to be flat MoM in October and decline

sequentially in November as order momentum is weaker-than-expected.
FAU/CPO development well on track: First FA  project passed customer
verification; expanding from FA into FAU, including coupling and potential FAU-to-
carrier assembly, with a dedicated new facility expected to support mass production
from around mid-2027. We maintain our long-term positive view on Largan's FAU/
CPO opportunities.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Largan Precision (3008.TW)
Base case: Our price target is based on an SOTP valuation, comprising the traditional business
and potential FAU opportunity. We value the traditional business using a residual income
model, consistent with our Greater China Hardware Tech coverage, assuming an 8.5% cost of
equity, a 7% net profit CAGR over 2026-36E and a 3% terminal growth rate. We separately

value the FAU business, assuming a 40% market share in 2028.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected high-end smartphone demand
n Faster-than-expected penetration of periscopes
n Stronger-than-expected CPO volume growth
Risks to Downside
n Greater-than-expected competition, which would erode Largan's ASP and gross margin
n Weaker-than-expected smartphone demand, especially for high-end models
n Market share gains and more pricing pressure from Sunny

n Weaker-than-expected CPO volume growth

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

New capacity announcements do not overturn our bullish view.
AI demand and increasing substrate complexity should keep the
market undersupplied from 2027, supporting further pricing and
margin expansion. We raise earnings estimates and price targets
for Unimicron and NYPCB and reiterate OW on both.
More capacity, but limited near-term relief: Suppliers are stepping up investment,

but several newly announced projects are not scheduled to ramp until late 2028-30
or beyond. Our updated supply-demand model continues to indicate undersupply
from 2027 through 2030, with tighter supply in CY27-28, which should only be
more favorable to pricing. Therefore, capacity additions announced in the past few
months do not, in our view, diminish the magnitude of pricing gains over the next
two years.
CY27 pricing should accelerate, amid tighter supply-demand in CY27-28, but

visibility on the magnitude remains limited: We see ABF substrate prices rising
25%+ in CY27, below buy-side expectations of ~50%. Our supply/demand outlook
points to undersupply next year, with larger substrates, higher layer counts, and
greater manufacturing complexity further constraining usable output. Raw material
cost pass-through was a key driver of CY26 price increases, we expect supply
tightness to play a greater role in CY27. This should allow pricing to outpace cost

inflation, driving faster margin expansion. We remain constructive on pricing, but the
magnitude and timing of increases depends on customer-level negotiations.
With pricing a segment-wide tailwind, we prefer Unimicron for its stronger CY27
capacity growth: As supply tightens, the ability to deliver incremental volume
should become a key differentiator. We favor suppliers bringing capacity online in
CY27 over those whose expansion plans contribute in later years. Unimicron’s

Guangfu II ramp should add 10-15% to capacity in CY27, compared with 5-10%
growth at NYPCB through debottlenecking, giving Unimicron greater scope to
benefit from both pricing and volume growth. Unimicron is also our preferred play
heading into earnings, as more demanding expectations for NYPCB leave less room
for an upside surprise. But we remain OW on NYPCB given its networking and AMD
CPU exposure, alongside its proactive pricing strategy.
We reiterate Overweight on Unimicron and NYPCB: Reflecting stronger earnings

trajectories, we raise our EPS estimates for both companies. Based on upside to our
revised price targets, our order of preference is Unimicron (NT$1,775 PT; 24x CY28e
P/E; 41% upside), followed by NYPCB (NT$2,000 PT; 22x CY28e P/E; 39% upside).
Potential risks: 1) Weaker demand for PCs, general servers, and AI servers; 2)
meaningful capacity expansion plans; 3) T-glass shortage; 4) CoWoP displacing ABF
substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
October 14, 2026 08:00 AM GMT

Supply/Demand Update: Undersupply Deepens in CY27–28
Our updated model points to a wider supply shortfall in CY27–28, despite additional
capacity: Incorporating the latest expansion plans and semiconductor demand forecasts,
we now expect ABF substrate demand to exceed available supply by 9.3% in CY27 and
13.7% in CY28, versus 6.2% and 9.4% previously. This represents a widening of 3.1ppt and
4.3ppt, respectively, relative to our prior estimates. We maintain our view that CY26 is a

broadly balanced market, followed by increasingly tight supply over the next two years.
The main demand upgrade comes from other ASICs, including Chinese AI chips: We
raise our shipment assumptions for this category to 6.4mn units in CY27 and 8.7mn in
CY28, from 4.6mn and 6.2mn previously. Our Nvidia GPU and server CPU forecasts remain
largely unchanged, while we lower AMD GPU estimates while also keeping CY27 Trainium
and TPU shipments largely unchanged. The tighter substrate outlook therefore reflects

stronger demand across a broader set of ASIC suppliers, rather than higher shipment
assumptions for every major AI platform. We also raise our substrate content assumptions
for select chips based on our latest supply chain checks, lifting our substrate demand
estimates across some segments, including server CPUs and networking.
Higher supply assumptions provide limited relief in CY27-28: We now include additional
capacity announcements that have been announced recently, including two additional

Kinsus factories, scheduled to come online in CY28 and CY29, beyond the CY27 expansion
already in our model. Several larger projects, however, contribute later. NYPCB’s new site
starts ramping in 2H29, while SEMCO’s Sejong expansion begins mass production in
September 2028 and its project in Vietnam is scheduled to begin operations by June
2030. These additions increase medium-term supply but do not prevent the modeled
shortfall from worsening through CY28.

Increasing substrate complexity adds to the pressure on usable output: Larger body
sizes, higher layer counts, and more demanding manufacturing processes constrain the
output available to meet demand, even as suppliers expand installed capacity. With the
supply gap widening in CY27-28, we expect tightness to become a more important pricing
driver than raw material cost pass-through. This supports our forecast for 25%+ price
increases in CY27 and further increases in CY28, alongside continued margin expansion.

Supply-side developments
Unimicron (3037.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On September 30, Unimicron announced that it has acquired a Hukou site from
Bridgestone Taiwan for ~NT$10.089bn (~US$318mn). Based on our checks, this is for
capacity expansion plans in 2030 or beyond.
For CY26, Unimicron has announced capex of NT$53.7B.

Over the next few years, Unimicron already has several new production facilities coming
online, including:
•  Guangfu II: ramp in 2027
• Yangmei II: ramp in 2028
• Yangmei III: ramp in 2029
• Yangmei IV: ramp in 2029+
• Bridgestone: ramp in 2030+

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
NYPCB (8046.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On August 6, NYPCB announced that its Board of Directors had approved a capex budget
of NT$46.8bn (~US$1,474mn) to invest in building a new ABF substrate production site.
Our checks indicate that this production site is located in the southern part of Taiwan, and
will start to ramp up production in 2H29. Given the size of this investment, we estimate
that it will approximately double its capacity exiting CY30, relative to its CY28 capacity.

Kinsus (3189.TW, Not Covered)
In our previous model, we had assumed only one factory (k6b) coming online in CY27,
increasing capacity ~30%. However, recent checks suggest that Kinsus intends to bring
two more factories online, one in CY28 (k6c) and one in CY29 (k6d). This implies that
capacity would increase another ~25% in CY28 and ~20% in CY29, bringing Kinsus' total
ABF substrate capacity to almost double exiting CY29, relative to its CY26 capacity.

Ibiden (4062.T, covered by Shoji Sato)
Back in February, Ibiden first announced that it will invest capex of ¥500bn in the
electronics field planned through F3/29, for ABF substrate expansion. Sato-san's latest
view is that Ibiden will need a new factory building to install the equipment and facilities
that will be purchased with the ¥500bn. As a result, Sato-san expects Ibiden during its 2Q
results briefing (October 30) to announce plans to make a large capital investment,

including for a factory building with a long lead time, and raise its medium-term earnings
forecast (report here).
SEMCO (009150.KS, covered by Shawn Kim)
On September 29, SEMCO announced investment of KRW4.27 trillion (~US$3,178mn) in
Sejong and KRW2.51 trillion (~US$1,488mn) in Vietnam, for AI server semiconductor
substrate capacity expansion.
• Sejong: Investment period from September 2026 to May 2028, with mass
production starting in September 2028. Largest single-product investment to date,

serving as key hub for mass production of high-value-added, highly demanding
products, proactively responding to the increasing demand for AI server substrates.
• Vietnam: The Vietnamese subsidiary aims to complete construction and begin
operations by June 2030. It will be operated as a strategic hub to respond to global
volume demand and enhance production efficiency through a stable supply
system.
With this investment, Samsung Electro-Mechanics plans to actively respond to the

growing demand for high-value-added semiconductor substrates in high-growth markets
such as AI servers, HPC, and automotive electronics. In particular, this investment is being
pursued based on medium- to long-term cooperation with global big tech and
semiconductor customers. The investment includes customer funding support and
medium- to long-term volume guarantees; thus, the company expects to strengthen the
competitiveness of its FCBGA business based on a stable demand foundation.

LG Innotek (011070.KS, covered by Ryan Kim)

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
According to news reports (link), LG Innotek signed a memorandum of understanding
(MOU) with Haiphong, Vietnam, for an investment to expand its semiconductor substrate
plant. Under the agreement, LG Innotek will push to build a semiconductor substrate
plant in Haiphong.
•  Construction will begin in July 2026 and is scheduled to be completed in May
2027.
• The site spans approximately 330,000 square meters (98,000 pyeong), equivalent

to 45 soccer fields.
• According to a news report, LG Innotek will invest approximately $1 billion, or
about 1.53 trillion won, for its Phase 1 expansion plan, which mainly includes RF-SiP
(radio frequency system-in-package) and FC-CSP (flip chip-chip scale package).
• ABF substrates will be part of the Phase 2 expansion plan, but no details or
investment size has been disclosed, yet.
According to a press release from LG Innotek on September 30, LG Innotek started mass

production of ABF substrates for PC chipsets targeting big tech clients last year. It
initiated high-volume mass production of FC-BGA for PC CPUs in the third quarter of this
year. Simultaneously, the company is rapidly boosting revenue by securing additional
major clients. Starting next year, it plans to sequentially enter the AI accelerator and AI
server markets.
• Revenue from LG Innotek's package solution business has maintained an upward
trajectory for three consecutive years. With the package solution business

demonstrating tangible results alongside its optical solution business, the
company has strengthened its capacity to generate stable earnings.
• LG Innotek plans to rapidly expand its market share in the emerging semiconductor
substrate market, aiming to build its package solution business, including
semiconductor substrates, into a business generating US$743 million in operating
profit by 2031.
• In the future, LG Innotek plans to develop its future businesses, including

semiconductor substrates and physical AI solutions, to US$5.94 billion by 2030.
The company aims to have such future new businesses account for 25% of total
revenue, thereby completing a well-balanced business portfolio.
The expanded plant will produce major semiconductor substrates including radio
frequency system-in-package (RF-SiP), flip chip chip scale package (FC-CSP), and flip chip
ball grid array (FC-BGA). "The Gumi site will serve as a 'mother factory' responsible for

developing new semiconductor substrate technologies and producing new models and
high-value-added products, while the expanded plant in Vietnam will be operated as a
production base for general-purpose semiconductor substrates," an LG Innotek official
explained.
AT&S (ATSV.VI, Not Covered)
On June 15, AT&S announced that it had signed key terms with AMD and Marvell to add
AI/HPC IC substrate capacity in Kulim, Malaysia. The company said the €1.5-2bn

investment (approximately US$1.68bn to US$2.24bn) is fully supported and financed by
long-term customer commitments, pending final execution.

Shinko (private)
On September 30, Shinko announced that it has entered into a purchase agreement with
Japan Display Inc. (JDI) to acquire its Mobara fab. In addition, it has entered into a sale
agreement with FICT Ltd. for the transfer of a portion of the fab to be acquired.
• Shinko will position the fab as a new core plant for advanced semiconductor
packages, and strengthen its production systems for flip-chip type packages
through the determination and implementation of capital investment plans for the

fab in conjunction with capacity expansion at its plants in the Nagano area.
Through these initiatives, Shinko aims to respond accurately to strong market
demand while pursuing sustainable business growth and enhancing corporate
value.
• The JDI Mobara fab has a total land area of 373,483 square meters, with Shinko
using the eastern area of the Fab (140,738 square meters, or ~38%) and FICT
planning to use the western area (232,745 square meters, or ~62%).

Toppan (7911.T, Not Covered)
According to news, Toppan broke ground in March 2024 on a new factory on Pesawat
Drive near Singapore's Jurong Lake District, built through its local unit Advanced Substrate
Technologies (AST), a JV with Broadcom. It makes FC-BGA substrates, which is Toppan's
term for them. It is Toppan's first FC-BGA plant outside Japan. The plant opening
ceremony took place on September 30, 2026.
• AST operates a facility of approximately 70,000 m² in the vicinity of Jurong Lake

District and employs more than 200 highly skilled engineers and technicians.
• Focus on high-demand, large-size, high-layer-count substrates
• While the investment size is not disclosed directly by the company, Nikkei sized
the investment at around ¥50 billion (~US$317mn).
Daeduck (353200.KS, Not Covered)
Daeduck announced on July 20, 2026, that it is investing ₩497 billion (~US$370M)
manufacturing facilities and related infrastructure for semiconductor products, running

through December 31, 2027.
ABF capex is stepping up, but announced investment still falls short of implied
demand. We estimate that ABF substrate makers have announced ~US$14.3bn of
investment year to date, materially above the segment’s aggregate annual capex in recent
years, although these commitments span multiple years. Our tally is not exhaustive. LG
Innotek has yet to disclose the size of its planned ABF capacity investment, while Shinko
has not quantified the additional spending required to expand capacity following its

announced acquisition of a JDI fab.
Our supply chain checks suggest that each US$1 of investment can support US$0.50–
0.60 of annual revenue at full ramp. On this basis, the US$14.3bn announced so far could
support US$7.2–8.6bn of incremental annual revenue. This compares with US$11.4bn of
incremental revenue implied by our industry forecast. We model the ABF substrate
market reaching US$20.9bn in 2030, up from US$9.5bn in 2026. Despite the step-up in

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
capex announcements, the investments we can currently quantify appear insufficient to
meet our demand forecast, pointing to a need for further capacity expansion over the
coming years.

Stronger ASIC demand from hyperscalers
We have updated our ASIC demand forecasts in our ABF model to align with the estimates

from our Semiconductors team. As shown in the exhibits below, our revised assumptions
point to a higher estimate for Amazon's Trainium processors in 2026, as well as marginally
lowered shipment estimates for Amazon's Trainium processors and Google's TPUs in 2027,
while the other  estimates  stay largely unchanged. These ASICs are characterized by large
die sizes and high layer-count package substrates, and thus we believe the slightly
lowered 2027 volume estimates do not derail our constructive outlook for the substrate

suppliers.

3Q preview: Morgan Stanley estimates vs. consensus
Unimicron
We expect a slightly stronger gross margin of 27.6%, ~30bps above the Street estimate,
reflecting a more favorable product mix and improving pricing environment. On operating

expenses, our estimate is roughly the same as consensus, resulting in an operating margin
of 19.4%, ~70bps above the Street estimate. However, our non-op income estimate of NT
$661mn is materially higher than the Street's estimate of NT$1,495mn in non-op loss. This
leads to our EPS estimate of NT$5.21 being ~37% higher than the Street.

The State of the Current Cycle
The ABF substrate market has gone through a few cycles over the past two
decades. We believe we are now still in the early innings of an up-cycle.
There was a prolonged down-cycle from 2012-18 as the PC TAM shrank amid the rise of
smartphones and tablets. During this tough period, companies were not able to generate
profits and had to compete on price to fill their idle capacity. As a result, during those

years, there was no or very limited capacity expansion, and capital expenditures by the
major ABF substrate suppliers were kept at a minimum.
Even as general server demand picked up, lifting utilization rates across the group going
into 2020, ABF substrate suppliers remained cautious on the longer-term demand outlook
and remained disciplined, hesitating to expand capacity. Hence, capex turned up just
slightly in 2019-20, increasing ~US$600mn vs. ~US$2.2bn on average per year in 2012-18.

This underinvestment led to sudden undersupply, as the shock from Covid-19 drove strong
work-from-home demand for PCs, as well as increased data usage and storage for general
computing driven by online entertainment. This ultimately led to a significant increase in
ABF substrate market value from 2020-22, reflected by the sudden surge in demand
along with meaningful pricing adjustments owing to the lack of supply.
During this 2020-22 period, much capacity expansion was set in motion as ABF substrate

suppliers and customers came to agree that necessary investments were needed for
future growth. Some subsidies were even given to these suppliers to facilitate this
capacity expansion. This is why capex jumped from ~US$2.9bn in 2020 to US$4.1bn in
2021 and US$5.5bn in 2022; even in 2023-24, capex remained elevated, as capacity
expansion plans continued.
Unfortunately, demand did not play out as expected. As the world came out of the
pandemic, demand for PCs and online entertainment fell sharply. This normalization of

demand, coupled with capacity expansion from practically every major ABF substrate
supplier, resulted in the ABF substrate market once again becoming oversupplied. This led
to a sharp decline in ABF substrate market value in 2023-24, as volumes normalized,
driving prices down to normalize against the new supply/demand dynamic.
Fortunately, cloud demand remained strong for general computing, and with AI demand
starting to pick up, the down-cycle this time around was much shorter (two years) vs. the

prior down-cycle (six years).
In 1H24, the ABF substrate market troughed and we saw an upward inflection since then,
but the pace of the recovery has been moderate and bifurcated. Midrange/low-end
markets such as PCs have shown moderate demand recovery, with high-end server and AI-
related end-demand recovering and growing at a faster pace. This is why we turned the
page on our bearish stance in 1H25, but did not turn more bullish until early 2026.

14
Periods Description
Period I (2010-2012): PC-centric
equilibrium
ABF substrate demand is primarily driven and dominated by CPU and GPU demand for desktops, notebooks,
discrete GPUs, with a meaningfully smaller exposure towards server CPUs.
Period II (2012-16): PC decline
and demand dilution
Global PC demand entered  a structural down-cycle as consumer behavior changed, with smartphones and tablets
absorbing more consumer time spent. This resulted in lower volumes and increased pricing competition as

suppliers fought to fill idle capacity.
Period III (2016-18): The quiet
inflection - servers and early
HPC
During these years, the PC market matured, while cloud computing started to take off. At the same time, server
CPU substrates quietly grew larger with more layers. We also started to see early accelerators being adopted in
high performance computing.
Period IV (2018-2022): WFH
demand driven structural
shortage
The Covid-19 pandemic drove massive work from home demand for consumer electronics and cloud computing.

This sudden surge in PC demand and increased time spent on gaming and online entertainment, bundled with a
continuous increase in substrate package size and layer count, resulted in a structural shortage, which also resulted
in longer lead times and raised prices. It was during this period (2021-22) that ABF suppliers globally started to
invest in new capacity.
Period V (2022-2024): Post-
pandemic correction and new
capacity arrival
As the world came out of the Covid-19 pandemic, WFH demand faded and  PC and cloud computing demand were

negatively affected. But at the same time, the new investments and capacity additions were just starting to slowly
come online, resulting in a drop in utilization rates and pricing.
Period VI (2024-2026):
Bifurcated recovery
PC demand normalized at the pre-Covid levels, with some replacement demand driven by Windows upgrade, while
the recovery and growth of AI and general server demand for cloud picks up at a meaningfully higher growth
trajectory.

Period VII (2026-30): AI diffusion Demand for AI proliferates from training to inference, driving ABF substrate market growth to re-accelerate over the
next five years after a two-year down-cycle following the Covid-19 pandemic. This drives increased demand for AI
computing, networking, and cloud computing, while demand for PCs remains soft.

Breaking down ABF substrate end demand
The end market mix for ABF substrates has shifted dramatically over the past decade. PCs

were once the dominant demand driver, accounting for approximately 70% of global ABF
substrate market value in 2015, supported primarily by PC CPUs and GPUs. Since then,
however, the industry's demand profile has undergone a structural transformation. We
estimate that PC-related applications represented only ~23% of total ABF substrate
market value in 2025, and this share is expected to decline further to below 10% by 2030.
In contrast, demand from servers, AI GPUs, AI ASICs, and networking applications has

expanded rapidly. We estimate that these segments collectively accounted for only
around 10% of the ABF substrate market in 2015, but their share increased to ~60% by
2025. Looking ahead, we expect they will represent more than 80% of total ABF
substrate market value by 2030.
In our view, this structural shift in end-market demand has been the primary driver of ABF
substrate industry growth over the past decade and will become even more pronounced

over the next five years. As AI infrastructure deployment accelerates, we expect continued
expansion in server, AI accelerator, and networking demand to drive meaningful
acceleration in ABF substrate market growth.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our 12-month price target increases ~21% to NT$1,775 (from NT$1,465), driven by our
increased earnings estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have
returned to a growth track from 2026 onward and will show stronger  growth in 2027,
driven by the high-end ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply and Unimicron's
dominant supply share in AI ASICs and also server CPUs, which puts it in a favorable

position to benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and see growth
accelerating from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs, and networking chips. New
technologies such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T should also increase the
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, which adds to the under-capacity as

usable output decreases.
Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value Unimicron – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm as it takes into account cost of equity. Our key assumptions
include:
• Cost of equity of 9.2% (unchanged)
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 14% (slightly lowered from 15%)

• Terminal growth rate of 3% (unchanged, similar to the rest of the companies under
our tech hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,030 and NT
$595, respectively.

Morgan Stanley Research 23
Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~29%, to NT$2,000, reflecting our raised earnings
estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have returned to a growth
track from 2026 onward and will show stronger growth in 2027, driven by the high-end
ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply, NYPCB's dominant supply share for
networking chips, and its proactive pricing strategy, which puts it in a favorable position to

benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and growth should
accelerate from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs and networking chips. New
technologies, such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T, should also increase
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, adding to the overall undercapacity
as usable output decreases.

Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value NYPCB – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. Key assumptions
(all unchanged):
• Cost of equity of 9.3%;
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 17%  –  fair given our analysis of the substrate market

over the next couple of years;
• Terminal growth rate of 3% (similar to the rest of the companies under our tech
hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,575 and NT
$1,020, respectively.

MS Rating
▪ We expect the ABF substrate market to
revert to undersupply from 2027 onward,
with the undersupply gap reaching 26% by
2030.
▪ This is primarily driven by AI demand, with
next-gen AI/networking chips adopting
larger body size ABF substrates.
▪ In the near to medium term, the short
supply of T-glass will be a key watch point,
but we believe this will mainly affect low-

end ABF and BT substrates, as suppliers
prioritize supply to the AI chip vendors.
▪ Our price target implies 41x 2027e P/E or
24x 2028e P/E, above the 10-20x range at
which it traded in the previous up-cycle. We
deem this fair, given AI chip substrate spec
upgrades and suppliers' ability to raise
prices.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 196: 9月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ ～FRBは年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ ～FRBは年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

FED  Watching

2026 年 10 月 8 日

1/5 
9 月ＦＯＭＣ議事録：タカ派姿勢に滲む「データ依存」の慎重さ 
～FRB は年内追加利上げを視野に入れるも、決定は今後の経済指標次第～ 
 経済調査部 主任エコノミスト  桂畑 誠治  
（℡：050-5474-7493）

要旨  
・9 月FOMCでは全会一致（12 対 0）で 0.25%の利上げを決定。非投票メンバーを含む参加者 
全 19 人が利上げに賛成した。 
・経済・金利見通し（SEP）提出者の大半が年内（10 月または 12 月）の更なる利上げを見込 
んでおり、全体としてタカ派的トーンが際立つ。 
・インフレリスクは上振れ傾向にあるものの、中長期のインフレ予想は 2%目標水準に安定。 
FRBは毎会合のデータ次第で柔軟に判断する「オープンな姿勢」を維持している。 
・今後の見通しとしては、景気や労働市場が安定する中でコアCPIやコアPCEの鈍化が確認され 
れば、予防的な追加利上げが見送られる可能性がある（最終決定は 12 月FOMCまでのデータ次
第） 。

１． 9 月FOMC ：参加者 19 人全員 が利上げ に賛成  
ケビン・ウォーシュFRB議長の新体制下で初めての利上げが決定された 9 月FOMCの議事録が公表 
された。新体制では会合直後の声明文のテキスト量が大幅に削減されたほか、記者会見時間も短
縮され、直接的な情報発信量が絞り込まれている。そうした中で、後日公表される「議事録」の
情報量は引き続き高水準に維持されており、FRBの本音を推し量る最重要ツールとしての重みが増
している。ただし、公表がFOMC終了から 3 週間後となるため、情報の遅効性には注意が必要であ 
る。 
9 月の利上げ決定については、投票権のないメンバーも含めた参加者全員（19 人）が利上げに賛
成した。さらに、経済・金利予測（SEP：議長自身は今回も SEP を提出していない）で示されていた 
通り、SEP を提出した 18 人の参加者のうち大半が「年内（10 月 27-28 日または 12 月 15-16 日）に
更なる利上げが適切となる可能性が高い」と見解を示しており、インフレへの強い警戒感を示すタ 
カ派（金融引き締め寄り）なトーンが際立つものとなった。ただし、委員会として今後の政策判断 
は新たに入手されるデータに依存（Data-dependent）するスタンスを明確に維持している。

２．労働市場は完全雇用水準で安定、インフレには上振れリスク 
委員会は全会一致（12 対 0）で、FF金利誘導目標レンジを 0.25％ポイント引き上げ、3.75％〜

### Page 2

2/5 
4.00％とすることを決定した。労働市場が完全雇用に近い水準で安定している一方で、インフレ 
率が高止まりしていることが背景にある。 
インフレの判断では、参加者は共通して、 「インフレ率は高止まりしており、ここ数ヶ月でイン 
フレ抑制に向けた十分な進展は見られていない」と指摘した。 「原油や精製燃料製品の価格を押し 
上げた継続的な地政学的動向や、急増するAI関連投資がインフレ圧力に寄与している」との認識 
が示された。 
複数の参加者がコア財・コアサービス価格の上振れや、関税の影響に言及したほか、数名の参 
加者がPCE指数の算出手法改定による影響や、3 ヶ月年率換算のインフレ率が年後半にインフレを 
過小評価しやすい傾向に留意すべきだと警鐘を鳴らした。一方で参加者全員が、 「中長期のインフ 
レ予想に関する市場ベースおよび調査ベースの指標が、委員会の 2％目標と一致する水準を維持し 
ている」と判断しており、追加利上げを過度に急ぐ必要性は示されなかった。 
労働市場に関しては、参加者は概ね「労働市場の状況が安定している」と判断し、 「完全雇用に 
近い状態にある」と評価した。 「失業率が現在の水準付近にとどまり、労働市場の状況は引き続き 
安定する」と予想した。一方で、数名の参加者が低水準の採用・解雇率など流動性の低下を指摘 
し、一部の参加者がAI専門職における局所的な賃金上昇圧力に触れた。そのうえで、参加者は概
ね「労働市場に対する上振れリスクと下振れリスクはほぼ均衡している」と評価した。 
経済活動に関しても、参加者は一般に「堅調なペースで拡大している」と評価。 「地政学的動向 
による供給面での悪影響が強まったものの、旺盛な設備投資と底堅い個人消費が経済活動を支え 
ている」との判断が示された。なお、一部の参加者からは、AI関連投資が設備投資と潜在生産性 
を押し上げる一方で中期的に総需要が総供給を上回るリスクや、株高などを背景とする金融環境 
の緩和的状況、高所得層と低・中所得層における消費の二極化構造への懸念が指摘された。 
以上のように、参加者は共通して「労働市場が完全雇用に近く力強さの兆候も見られる一方で 
インフレ率は高止まりしており、経済活動が堅調なペースで拡大していること」を強調した。さ
らに、ほぼすべての参加者が、 「インフレリスクは上振れに傾いているものの、労働市場に対する 
リスクは減少し、現在はほぼ均衡している」と評価した。こうした経済見通しと変化するリスク 
バランスに基づき、全参加者（19 人）がFF金利誘導目標レンジの引き上げが適切であると判断し
た。

3．政策金利の水準感とリスク管理の姿勢 
今回の議論において注目されるのは、利上げの根拠と政策金利水準に対する見解の多様さであ 
る。多くの参加者は、予想以上の需要や供給面での悪影響によりインフレ率が持続的に目標を上 
回り続けることに対する保険（Insurance）を提供するため、誘導目標レンジを高めの軌道とする 
ことがリスク管理上の観点から賢明であると強調した。 
一方で、相当数の参加者はリスク管理上の観点からではなく、自身の標準的な見通し（Modal 
outlooks）に基づいて高めの目標軌道が必要であると見なした。さらに、数名の参加者は中立金 
利の推計値を引き上げたことに触れ、現在の政策金利は「緊縮的ではない、あるいは極めて緩や

かに緊縮的であるに過ぎない」とのタカ派的な認識を示した。これは、今後の引き締め継続を正
当化する材料となる。 
また、バランスシート政策に関しては、数名の参加者が国債市場の円滑な機能を認めつつも、 
将来の市場不全に備えたツールやコミュニケーションの強化を提案したことが確認された。

４．先行きに対する見通し 
今回の会合以降の金融政策の見通しに関して、大半の参加者（SEP提出者 18 人のうち）が年末 
までにFF金利誘導目標レンジを再度引き上げることが適切となる可能性が高いとの見方を示し 
た。ただし、全員が共通のスタンスとしてオープンな姿勢で各会合に臨み、今後の決定は新たに 
入手する情報と、それが経済見通しおよびリスクバランスに与える含意に依存することを強調、
毎会合ごとに判断されることを示し、政策の自由度を維持した。 
今回の議事録は全体として「年内追加利上げ」に前向きなタカ派的トーンが目立ったものの、 
インフレ予想の安定や「毎会合データ次第」というスタンスから、FRBが無理な連続利上げを焦っ 
ているわけではないことが示された。今後の金融政策の焦点を左右するのは、10 月・12 月FOMCま
でに発表される景気、労働市場や、コアCPI、コアPCEの動向である。景気や労働市場の安定が続 
くと予想されるもとで、インフレの鈍化傾向が確認される場合、予防的な追加利上げは見送られ 
る可能性がある（最終決定は今後のデータ次第） 。

### Page 1

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: From Sep/3Q26 Taiwan semi/tech sales to see signs of global semi/technology

From Sep/3Q26 Taiwan
semi/tech sales to see signs
of global semi/technology
從台灣科技及半導體  3Q26 營收找全球科技及半導體產業景氣趨勢
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
OCT 12 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
Executive summary

台灣半導體暨科技產業公布 9 ⽉份及 3Q26 營收，我們以公布的數據看產業趨勢
變化及公司與彭博社分析師預期進⾏分析⽐較，投資⼈須審慎評估。總⽽⾔
之， 3Q26 營收明顯超過彭博社分析師預期的公司⼤幅多於低於預期的公司達 6
倍多，⽽超過 53% 年增（⽤晶圓代⼯的 9 ⽉份營收年增做標竿）的有 6 個次產
業。除此以外，我們還提供了 3Q26 營收年增明顯改善及惡化的前 20 名公司整
理及排序，及 3Q26 營收明顯季增 / 季減的前 20 名公司整理及排序。總結⽽
⾔，伺服器產業鏈（對標公司有 Nvidia, AMD, Intel, ⾕歌 TPU ， Dell,
SuperMicro) ，存儲記憶體（對標公司有三星 , SK Hynix, SanDisk,
Western Digital) ， CCL/PCB ，半導體建廠及測試產業 3Q26 營收（對標公司
有  Teradyne, Advantest, Form Factor ）明顯⾼於分析師預期， 3Q26 營收
年增改善明顯的有存儲記憶體，光纖通訊（對標公司有 Lumentum,

Fabrinet, Coherent ）， CoWoS/CoPoS 先進封測設備產業。 3Q26 營收季增
明顯改善的產業有 CoWoS/CoPoS 先進封測設備產業，設計服務（對標公司有
Broadcom, Marvell) ，存儲記憶體，光纖通訊，及伺服器產業鏈。

### Page 2

• The global landscape remains complex, shaped by AI, 
energy prices and tariffs. 
• Taiwan’s AI cycle remains strong, with robust AI 
exports and investment and an intact semiconductor 
competitive moat. 
• AI is generating broader domestic spillovers, with 
wealth effects and wage growth supporting 
consumption. 
• Taiwan is cushioning the energy shock by diversifying 
energy imports and absorbing much of the increase in 
upstream costs. 
• AI-driven growth is likely to moderate from 4Q26 into 
2027, as hyperscaler  cash-flow pressures rise and 
higher global rates lift investment costs. 
• Energy remains a n inflation risk, with domestic 
electricity prices facing upward pressure after the 
November local elections. 
• GDP growth is expected to moderate to high -single 
digits from 4Q26, while we maintain our 11.6%/5.6% 
GDP forecasts for 2026/27. 
• Inflation is likely to remain above 2% in 4Q26, with our 
2.0%/2.1% CPI forecasts for 2026/27 unchanged. 
• We maintain our call for CBC to hike 12.5bp to 2.125% 
in December, amid tighter liquidity conditions. 
• Housing should recover gradually despite credit 
easing, as excess new -home inventory remains a n 
overhang.

Taiwan: AI, energy and 4Q outlook

13 October 2026

Ma Tieying, CFA                        
Senior Economist 
matieying@dbs.com

### Page 2

Taiwan: AI, energy and 4Q outlook October 13, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

Global backdrop

The global landscape remains complex, 
shaped by the interplay of AI, energy 
prices and tariffs, with significant 
implications for growth, inflation and 
financial conditions.

1) AI

The debate over the durability of the AI 
investment boom continues.  Tailwinds 
include technological advances, broader 
adoption, and s trategic and geopolitical 
considerations. Headwinds range from 
uncertainty over investment returns  and 
hyperscaler financing pressure to 
physical bottlenecks , safety concerns, 
and regulatory uncertainty.

Hyperscaler financing pressure has 
attracted increasing attention . In the 
early stages of the AI investment boom, 
hyperscaler capex was largely financed 
by internal cash flow. This is now 
changing, as free cash flow is expected 
to turn negative from 4Q, requiring major 
players to rely on debt issuance and 
other external financing increasingly.

At the same time, higher global interest 
rates are raising the cost of AI 
investment. The Fed resumed rate hikes 
at its September meeting, while global 
bond yields remain under upward 
pressure amid persistent inflation and 
elevated public and private borro wing 
needs. Our baseline assumes US rates 
will rise by 50bps this year and a further 
25bps in 2027 to 4.50%, with global 
long-term rates also remaining under 
pressure. This could increasingly weigh 
on the economics and pace of AI 
investment.

US-China AI competition is also drawing 
increasing attention.  Across the five 
layers of the AI ecosystem, China is 
making rapid progress in energy, 
infrastructure, AI models and 
applications, supported by its large scale, 
cost-effective domestic manufacturing 
supply chains and huge consumer 
market, which provides a wide range of 
real-world scenarios for AI adoption. The 
emergence of a more integrated and 
cost-competitive Chinese AI ecosystem 
could increasingly challe nge the US 
ecosystem, which currently operates at a 
relatively higher cost.

China, however, remains relatively weak 
in semiconductors.  Around half of 
China's domestic AI chip market still 
relies on foreign suppliers, including 
lower-end NVIDIA chips. This 
semiconductor bottleneck remains a key 
constraint on China's AI development. In 
our view, Taiwanese semiconductor 
companies' competiti ve moat therefore 
remains largely intact in the near term.

to 39% in 2027. Similarly, global 
semiconductor sales remained strong 
through 3Q, while WSTS expects growth 
to moderate from 90% this year to 27 % 
in 2027.

2) Energy

The US -Iran conflict remains at a 
standoff, keeping the energy -price 
outlook highly uncertain.  New 
developments are likely after the US 
midterm elections in November, with 
both further escalation and de -
escalation possible. Crude oil prices have 
remained elevated at around 
USD100/bbl amid a persistent 
geopolitical risk premium . In contrast, 
refined product prices have risen more 
sharply as refining disruptions and 
tighter product logistics have amplified 
the supply shock. Natural gas prices 
have also increased sharply, reflecting 
disruptions to LNG supply and limited 
alternative shipping routes.

Taiwan has been actively diversifying 
its energy import sources to cushion the 
impact. LNG imports from Qatar have 
fallen to zero since April, while imports 
from the US and other suppliers have 
surged to offset the shortfall, helping 
maintain stable domestic electricity 
supply.

                                                                                                                                            5

3) Tariffs

US tariff actions are likely to accelerate 
after the US midterm elections in 
November. Two major tariff measures 
remain in the pipeline. The Section 301 
forced-labor tariff was implemented in 
July, while final tariff action under the 
Section 301 overcapacity investigation, 
which covers 16 economies, remains 
pending. Meanwhile, Phase 1 of the 
Section 232 semiconductor tariffs was 
implemented in January, while Phase 2, 
covering a broader range of 
semiconductor-related products, 
remains pending.

Taiwan has secured a relatively 
favourable framework through its trade 
and investment agreements with the 
US. Taiwan signed an Agreement on 
Reciprocal Trade (ART) and an 
Investment MOU with the US earlier this 
year. The ART establishes a 15% US tariff 
rate on Taiwanese goods, while the MOU 
provides Taiwan with preferential 
treatment under Section 232, including 
for semiconductors. The ART has yet to 
enter into force, and it remains to be 
seen how the final Section 301 tariff 
burden will interact with the 15% ART 
framework. Meanwhile, major Taiwanese 
companies such as TSMC are expanding 
their US investments under the 
Investment MOU, which could support 
relatively favourable treatment for 
Taiwan when broader Section 232 
semiconductor tariffs are introduced.

Taiwan 4Q outlook

Taiwan has navigated the complex 
global environment relatively well so far 
this year, supported by robust AI exports 
and investment, diversified energy 
imports, and efforts to absorb higher 
upstream energy costs. Some 
normalisation is likely in 4Q26 –2027, as 
the AI -driven growth boost moderates 
and domestic energy prices face greater 
pressure to adjust.

1) Growth

GDP growth remains broadly on track 
with our expectations. High -frequency 
indicators suggest that GDP growth 
should remain around 12% yoy in 3Q, 
broadly in line with 12.9% in 2Q but below 
the 15.4% peak in 1Q. Growth is expected 
to moderate to a high -single-digit pace 
from 4Q onwards. We maintain our full -
year GDP growth forecasts at 11.6% for 
2026 and 5.6% for 2027.

The AI cycle remains the key driver of 
Taiwan's growth.  Information and 
communication products and electronic 
components accounted for nearly 80% 
of total exports in the first nine months 
of this year, while manufacturing 
accounts for nearly 50% of gross fixed

capital formation. From 4Q onwards, 
export growth is expected to moderate 
from exceptionally strong levels to a 
more normal but still robust pace, 
broadly following the trajectory of 
global hyperscaler capex and 
semiconductor sales. Investment is likely 
to follow a similar path.

For now, Taiwanese technology 
companies continue to report robust 
revenue growth, led by semiconductors, 
ODMs/EMS, and PCs and servers. 
Capacity utilisation at leading Taiwanese 
foundries has also continued to rise, 
suggesting that investment demand 
remains strong.

The AI boom is also beginning to 
generate broader spillovers to the 
domestic economy. The AI-driven equity 
rally has generated significant wealth 
effects, with the TAIEX up 70% year-to-
date and Taiwan becoming the world's 
fifth-largest stock market. Taiwanese 
households have benefited from the 
appreciation of financial assets, with 
equities and mutual funds accounting for 
nearly 30% of total household financial 
assets. The equity rally has also 
generated positive income effects. Wage 
growth has picked up, led by the services 
sector, particularly financial services, as 
strong earnings at financial 
institutions—especially securities 
firms—have prompted higher 
compensation to retain talent. These 
wealth and income gains should support 
household consumption, creating a 
positive feedback loop that broadens the 
benefits of the AI boom across the wider 
economy.

                                                                                                                                            7

2) Inflation

CPI inflation is also tracking broadly in 
line with our forecast. Headline CPI 
remained elevated at 2.4% yoy in 3Q, up 
from 1.2% in 1Q and 2.2% in 2Q. We 
maintain our forecast for headline CPI 
inflation to remain close to 2.5% in 4Q, 
and retain our full-year forecasts at 2.0% 
for 2026 and 2.1% for 2027.

Energy inflation remains the main 
upside risk.  The government has 
absorbed much of the increase in energy 
costs through state -owned CPC and 
Taipower, limiting the pass -through to 
domestic consumers. With losses at both 
companies widening, the government 
will need to decide whether to provide 
additional subsidies to cover the losses 
or allow further increases in domestic 
energy prices. The September re view 
postponed the adjustment to the 
December meeting, when the overall 
situation will be reassessed after the 
November local elections. Taipower's  
latest review indicated that electricity 
rates would need to rise by 12.8%. A hike 
in electricity prices could lift inflation 
expectations and generate second -
round effects, particularly as the labour 
market tightens and wage pressures 
increase.

3) Monetary policy

The central bank kept the policy 
discount rate unchanged at 2.00% at its 
September meeting, in line with our 
expectations. We continue to expect a 
modest 12.5bp rate hike at the 
December meeting, bringing the policy 
rate to 2.125%.  By then, CPI inflation is 
likely to have remained above 2% for 
around six consecutive months. The 
possibility of an increase in electricity 
prices following the November local 
elections could further lift inflation 
expectations, strengthening the case for 
a December rate hike.

Financial conditions have already 
tightened despite the absence of a 
policy rate hike.  M2 growth remained 
around 7% yoy in 3Q, well below double-
digit nominal GDP growth. At the same 
time, bank loan growth has accelerated 
to more than 10% yoy, driven by strong

                                                                                                                                            8

corporate credit demand, particularly AI-
related fundraising by technology 
companies. Tighter financial conditions 
are also evident in rising market interest 
rates, including commercial paper rates 
and government bond yields.

The central bank has continued to ease 
housing credit controls gradually. At its 
September meeting, it raised the LTV 
cap on second -home mortgage loans to 
70%, the second easing of housing credit 
controls this year. There is scope for 
further easing, including removing the 
LTV cap on second-home loans or raising 
the LTV caps for third - and fourth-home 
loans and high -value properties, which 
currently stand at 30%. However, the 
central bank is likely to proceed 
cautiously, given concerns that a faster 
relaxation could reignite  speculative 
demand and put renewed upward 
pressure on housing prices.

4) Housing

A gradual and modest recovery in the 
housing market is expected from 4Q26 
into 2027.  The existing -home market 
has already shown early signs of 
recovery, with Sinyi  housing prices 
returning to slightly positive yoy growth 
since 2Q. Total building transactions also 
returned to positive yoy growth in 3Q. 
The easing of credit controls should help 
reinforce this modest recovery trend.

The new -home market remains under 
pressure. The number of new units under 
construction has started to decline since 
2023, while new completions are  only 
beginning to ease this year . Demand 
remains weak, as potential buyers 
continue to wait for lower prices. Unsold 
new homes stood at 113K units at end -
2025, compared with a historical normal 
level of around 80K, implying excess 
inventory of roughly 30K units. At the 
current pace of new -home transactions, 
it would take roughly one year to 
normalize the existing excess inventory, 
and longer when factoring in the new 
supply pipeline. Some industry experts 
estimate that it could take as long as 3.8 
years digest current inventories fully 
(here).

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường

07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 điểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 điểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao 
phục vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức 
cao cho phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện 
đáng kể trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm 
phán vẫn đang bế tắc và ít có khả năng dòng chảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối 
lại ngay trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi lựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đầu tư công, chính sách tiền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

I. Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
TTCK Việt Nam đi ngang, giảm nhẹ 
trong 9 tháng đầu năm 
 TTCK Việt Nam biến động giằng co, giảm điểm nhẹ trong 9 tháng đầu năm với 
diễn biến có thể chia làm 2 giai đoạn chính. Giai đoạn 1 kéo dài đến gần cuối 
quý 1, thị trường đi ngang trong 2 tháng đầu, trước khi lao dốc mạnh từ cuối 
tháng 2 trước ảnh hưởng bởi xung đột tại Trung Đông bùng phát. Giai đoạn 2 là 
khoảng thời gian còn lại, thị trường xuất hiện các nhịp biến động mạnh, 
tăng/giảm đan xen, chịu ảnh hưởng bởi thông tin về giá dầu, biến động lãi suất 
trong nước, kỳ vọng về nâng hạng thị trường và các yếu tố cơ bản khác. Tính 
chung 9 tháng, VNIndex gần như đi ngang, giảm nhẹ 0.9% so với cuối 2025, 
trong khi thanh khoản bình quân toàn thị trường giảm xấp xỉ 12% so với cùng 
kỳ.

### Page 4

4

Với diễn biến căng thẳng tại Trung Đông vẫn đang dai dẳng, đồng thời dự báo 
mặt bằng lãi suất sẽ khó xuất hiện xu hướng hạ nhiệt rõ nét trong năm 2026, dù 
vẫn có những điểm sáng vĩ mô và KQKD khả quan của doanh nghiệp, các dự 
báo của chúng tôi nhìn chung có phần thận trọng hơn so với dự báo gần nhất. 
Cụ thể:

Lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm 
yết dự báo tăng trưởng mạnh

Chúng tôi điều chỉnh dự báo tăng trưởng EPS toàn thị trường cho năm 2026 lên 
mức 33% YoY, chủ yếu nhờ phản ánh mức tăng 200% kế hoạch lợi nhuận sau 
thuế của VIC (loại trừ mức tăng trưởng đột biến này của VIC, tăng trưởng EPS 
toàn thị trường ở mức 17% YoY). Đóng góp chính đến từ mức tăng của một số 
nhóm có tỷ trọng lớn: Bất động sản (+79% YoY), Nguyên vật liệu (+44% YoY) và 
Hàng tiêu dùng thiết yếu (+27% YoY). Mức tăng trưởng EPS cao, tương đồng với 
số liệu tăng trưởng kinh tế vĩ mô tích cực, là động lực quan trọng hỗ trợ thị 
trường trong trung/dài hạn.

Bối cảnh vĩ mô quốc tế còn nhiều 
bất định

Bối cảnh kinh tế toàn cầu trong giai đoạn cuối năm vẫn chịu áp lực lớn từ xung 
đột địa chính trị ở Trung Đông và động thái điều hành chính sách của Fed. Đàm 
phán Mỹ – Iran chưa đạt tiến triển đáng kể, trong khi giao tranh tiếp diễn và 
hoạt động vận chuyển dầu bị gián đoạn, làm gia tăng rủi ro thiếu hụt nguồn 
cung và khiến giá dầu được dự báo tiếp tục neo cao trong thời gian tới. Giá 
năng lượng duy trì ở mức cao, cùng với áp lực lạm phát chưa hạ nhiệt rõ rệt, 
khiến Fed tiếp tục duy trì lập trường thận trọng và không loại trừ khả năng tiếp 
tục thắt chặt chính sách tiền tệ trong quý 4.

Mặt bằng lãi suất huy động dự báo 
duy trì mức cao trong quý 4

Chúng tôi duy trì đánh giá tích cực về triển vọng tăng trưởng kinh tế Việt Nam 
năm 2026, với GDP dự kiến tăng 8.5–9.0%, dựa trên kỳ vọng cán cân thương 
mại cải thiện, dòng vốn FDI tiếp tục hỗ trợ tăng trưởng và đầu tư công được đẩy 
mạnh. Tuy nhiên, giá năng lượng là rủi ro đáng chú ý, có thể gây thêm áp lực 
lên cán cân thương mại và lạm phát, qua đó ảnh hưởng đến triển vọng tăng 
trưởng trong thời gian tới. 
Áp lực thiếu hụt thanh khoản ngắn hạn đã có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt, trong khi áp 
lực huy động vốn trung và dài hạn vẫn hiện hữu khi nhu cầu vốn của nền kinh 
tế dự kiến ở mức cao. Do đó, nhìn về cuối năm, chúng tôi cho rằng lãi suất chưa 
thể xuất hiện xu hướng giảm rõ rệt, nhưng kỳ vọng có thể ổn định quanh mặt 
bằng hiện tại nhờ hoạt động giải ngân đầu tư công tiếp tục hỗ trợ dòng tiền và 
NHNN vẫn còn dư địa điều tiết thanh khoản trong bối cảnh áp lực tỷ giá không 
lớn và áp lực lạm phát vẫn trong tầm kiểm soát.

### Page 5

5

Biểu đồ 5. P/E forward 2026 các thị trường  Biểu đồ 6. Biến động EPS nhóm ASEAN4

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV  Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV

Định giá chỉ số VNIndex đang ở 
vùng hấp dẫn 
 P/E của chỉ số VNIndex hiện đang ở mức 13.5 (theo Bloomberg), thấp hơn đáng 
kể so với mức bình quân 5 năm gần nhất ở 15.1.  
Nếu loại trừ mức tăng đột biến ở nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup từ đầu năm 2025 đến 
nay, P/E của thị trường (sau đây gọi là P/E điều chỉnh) chỉ còn ở mức 10.9 lần. Đây 
là mức thấp hơn cả các đáy gần nhất trong quá khứ như đáy Covid vào tháng 
3/2020 (ở 11.3 lần), đáy của năm 2022 (ở 11.01 lần), và đáy thuế quan trong 
năm 2025 (ở 12 lần).

Lưu ý: chúng tôi tính toán P/E điều chỉnh với giả định P/E nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup 
giữ nguyên từ đầu năm 2025 (hay nói cách khác, biến động giá ở nhóm cổ 
phiếu này tương đồng với lợi nhuận trượt 4 quý). Cách tính này giúp loại bỏ ảnh 
hưởng của nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup lên VNIndex, khi mà riêng 2 cổ phiếu VIC, 
VHM đã đóng góp xấp xỉ 450 điểm cho mức tăng của chỉ số VNIndex từ đầu năm 
2025, và P/E của VIC hiện đang giao dịch ở mức 80 lần.

### Page 6

6

Chỉ số VNIndex được dự báo ở 
quanh mức 1,800 điểm thời điểm 
cuối năm 2026 
 Đối với triển vọng thị trường trong quý cuối năm 2026, với đánh giá mặt bằng 
lãi suất sẽ tiếp tục duy trì ở mức cao, kết hợp với xu hướng tăng trưởng lợi 
nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết vẫn sẽ được duy trì, chúng tôi dự báo vùng 
điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 1,800 điểm, tương 
đương mức tăng gần 2% so với đóng cửa quý 3. Dự báo này tương ứng:

- Mức tăng trưởng EPS 2026 của thị trường chung được kỳ vọng tăng 
mạnh lên mức 33% (tăng so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ 
yếu nhờ kỳ vọng vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và 
lợi nhuận tăng trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt 
động kinh doanh).

- Về mặt định giá, chúng tôi giảm nhẹ vùng P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIn-
dex thời điểm cuối năm xuống 13.2 (từ mức 13.5 ở thời điểm hiện tại - 
phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là vùng 
định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm, và vùng thấp lịch sử nếu loại bỏ 
đà tăng đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup từ đầu năm 2025 (thể hiện ở chỉ số 
P/E điều chỉnh mà chúng tôi nhận định ở trên). Đây là cơ sở để chúng tôi 
kỳ vọng thị trường sẽ có nhịp bật tăng mạnh ngay khi môi trường vĩ mô 
quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran) và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn đề lãi 
suất) chuyển biến theo chiều hướng tích cực. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi cho 
rằng các cải thiện này sẽ cần chờ đến nửa đầu 2027, vì vậy chúng tôi 
chưa nâng mức P/E hợp lý của thị trường ngay trong báo cáo này.

II. Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh

Chúng tôi nâng dự phóng tăng 
trưởng EPS bình quân các doanh 
nghiệp trên sàn HSX năm 2026 đạt 
33% YoY   
 Chúng tôi điều chỉnh dự báo tăng trưởng EPS toàn thị trường cho năm 2026 lên 
mức 33% YoY, chủ yếu nhờ phản ánh mức tăng đột biến lợi nhuận của VIC. Nếu 
loại trừ yếu tố đột biến này, tăng trưởng EPS đạt mức 17% YoY, với đóng góp 
chính đến từ mức tăng của một số nhóm có tỷ trọng lớn: Bất động sản (+79% 
YoY), Nguyên vật liệu (+44% YoY) và Hàng tiêu dùng thiết yếu (+27% YoY). 
Mức tăng trưởng EPS bình quân được duy trì 2 con số tiếp tục phản ánh kỳ vọng 
tích cực của chúng tôi về động lực tăng trưởng của Việt Nam trong năm nay vẫn 
được củng cố bởi đẩy mạnh đầu tư công, tăng trưởng ở khu vực tư nhân và 
dòng vốn FDI. Cụ thể, dự phóng của chúng tôi dựa trên một số trụ cột chính: 
- Khu vực kinh tế nhà nước tiên phong và đầu tư công tăng tốc: Nghị 
quyết 79 định hướng cho phép doanh nghiệp nhà nước giữ lại nguồn 
thu từ cổ phần hóa, thoái vốn và tăng tỷ lệ lợi nhuận để lại, qua đó 
giúp các tổng công ty niêm yết chủ động hơn về vốn cho các dự án 
trọng điểm. Về đầu tư công, giải ngân đạt gần 63% kế hoạch năm tính 
đến hết Q3/2026 – cải thiện so với mức 49.8% cùng kỳ, trong khi giải 
ngân vốn ngân sách trung ương chậm hơn ngân sách địa phương, cho 
thấy dư địa tăng tốc trong quý cuối năm còn lớn. Bên cạnh đó, Chính 
phủ tiếp tục quyết tâm đẩy mạnh các dự án hạ tầng chiến lược như: 
Sân bay Long Thành (dự kiến khai thác thương mại từ tháng 12/2026), 
Đường sắt Lào Cai – Hà Nội – Hải Phòng (đã giải ngân những dòng vốn 
đầu tiên trong 2026). Theo đó, chúng tôi kỳ vọng xây dựng hạ tầng và 
nguyên vật liệu tiếp tục hưởng lợi từ đẩy mạnh đầu tư công. 
- Khu vực kinh tế tư nhân được tạo điều kiện để tăng trưởng và nâng 
cao tính cạnh tranh: Nghị quyết 68 đang chuyển sang giai đoạn thực 
thi với các chính sách hỗ trợ cụ thể, cụ thể hóa qua Nghị quyết 198 và 
Nghị định 20. Song song với đó, việc đẩy mạnh cơ cấu lại vốn nhà nước 
đang mở rộng không gian để khu vực tư nhân tham gia sở hữu và phân 
bổ nguồn lực hiệu quả hơn, qua đó tạo điều kiện cho khu vực này mở 
rộng quy mô và tham gia sâu hơn vào nền kinh tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng 
tác động tích cực của chính sách là rõ nét trong trung – dài hạn.  
- Dòng vốn FDI tiếp tục tăng trưởng, tập trung vào công nghệ cao: 
Nghị quyết 10 định hướng nâng cao chất lượng dòng vốn FDI, ưu tiên 
công nghệ cao và tăng cường liên kết với doanh nghiệp trong nước. 
Chúng tôi kỳ vọng Việt Nam tiếp tục củng cố lợi thế cạnh tranh và giữ 
vững vị thế là điểm đến hấp dẫn của dòng vốn FDI, nhờ hưởng lợi kép 
từ: (i) xu hướng "China+1" giữa cạnh tranh công nghệ Mỹ – Trung; (ii) 
siêu chu kỳ công nghệ cao và AI thúc đẩy các tập đoàn lớn mở rộng 
đầu tư. Đáng chú ý, AI và bán dẫn đang nổi lên như động lực mới của 
chu kỳ FDI, kéo theo nhu cầu đầu tư lớn vào điện, truyền tải, hạ tầng 
số, khu công nghiệp và logistics. Theo đó, chúng tôi cho rằng cơ hội 
không chỉ nằm ở các doanh nghiệp công nghệ trực tiếp hưởng lợi, mà 
còn lan tỏa tới các doanh nghiệp giải quyết "nút thắt vật chất" để hấp 
thụ dòng vốn FDI công nghệ cao, đặc biệt trong lĩnh vực điện và xây 
lắp điện, khu công nghiệp, logistics và hạ tầng số.

### Page 8

Biểu đồ 9. Dự phóng tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV

Bất động sản, Nguyên vật liệu và 
Hàng tiêu dùng thiết yếu dẫn dắt 
tăng trưởng lợi nhuận 
 Dựa vào tình hình bối cảnh chung của kinh tế vĩ mô, đánh giá xu hướng ngành, 
kết hợp dự phóng cụ thể ở nhóm doanh nghiệp đầu ngành, chúng tôi đưa ra dự 
phóng như sau: 
- Các nhóm ngành dẫn dắt: Những lĩnh vực hưởng lợi chính trong xu 
hướng năm nay bao gồm Bất động sản (+79% YoY), Nguyên vật liệu 
(+44% YoY) và Hàng tiêu dùng thiết yếu (+27% YoY). Nhóm Bất động 
sản dự kiến có mức tăng đột biến (một phần nhờ tiếp tục ghi nhận 
từng phần doanh số bán hàng từ giai đoạn trước), chủ yếu phản ánh 
kết quả kinh doanh vượt trội của VIC và VHM. Nhóm Nguyên vật liệu 
được hưởng lợi từ làn sóng thúc đẩy đầu tư công và hạ tầng hoàn thiện 
khiến nhu cầu tăng đột biến, trong đó HPG là doanh nghiệp đóng góp 
nổi bật nhất. Nhóm Hàng tiêu dùng thiết yếu ghi nhận mức tăng 
trưởng ấn tượng, chủ yếu nhờ đóng góp của các doanh nghiệp đầu 
ngành hưởng lợi từ quá trình dịch chuyển tiêu dùng sang kênh bán lẻ 
hiện đại. 
- Các nhóm ngành tăng trưởng thấp hơn: Ngược lại, nhóm Công nghệ 
thông tin được dự báo có tốc độ tăng trưởng khiêm tốn hơn (+12% 
YoY). FPT — doanh nghiệp chiếm gần 90% tỷ trọng ngành — là nguyên 
nhân chính kéo tụt mức tăng trưởng chung, khi kết quả kinh doanh 
của doanh nghiệp này được dự phóng tăng trưởng khiêm tốn hơn mặt 
bằng chung. Trong khi đó, nhóm Công nghiệp được dự báo tăng 
trưởng âm nhẹ (-6% YoY), chủ yếu do dự phóng biên lợi nhuận suy 
giảm của nhóm Hàng không — VJC và HVN — trong bối cảnh giá dầu 
neo cao.

III. Bối cảnh vĩ mô và TTCK 
  1. Vĩ mô thế giới - Rủi ro duy trì 
Môi trường đầu tư quốc tế đầy biến 
động 
 Môi trường đầu tư toàn cầu đang trải qua giai đoạn nhiều biến động xoay 
quanh các vấn đề nóng như xung đột tại Trung Đông, giá dầu, lạm phát toàn 
cầu, chính sách tiền tệ của các NHTW lớn trên thế giới, chu kỳ bùng nổ đầu tư 
lĩnh vực AI… bên cạnh các rủi ro vẫn đang tiềm ẩn bùng phát trở lại như thuế 
quan, chiến tranh thương mại và các xung đột địa chính trị toàn cầu.

Trong tầm nhìn trung hạn, chúng tôi cho rằng 2 yếu tố cốt lõi có ảnh hưởng 
mạnh nhất đến TTCK Việt Nam sẽ là xung đột tại Iran và động thái điều hành 
của FED bởi: i) Giá dầu dự báo tiếp tục neo cao tạo áp lực lạm phát trong nước 
và hạn chế dư địa giảm lãi suất; ii) FED được dự báo còn 1 đợt tăng lãi suất 
trong năm nay nhằm hạ nhiệt rủi ro lạm phát.
